10.1.17
Proof of Lemma
9.17

Proof 10.17

Proof of Lemma
9.17
.
Note that we can re-write the sample variance estimator as

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}=\frac{1}{n(n-1)}\left(n\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2}-(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i})^{2}\right).

We now expand the variance of the sample variance estimator.

\displaystyle(\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n-1)^{2}}\left(n^{2}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2})^{2}-2n(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2})(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i})^{2}+(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i})^{4}\right)

Note that, the first term after taking expectation is

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2})^{2}\right]=n\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]+n(n-1)(\mathrm{E}[Y^{2}])^{2}.

The second term is

\mathrm{E}\left[(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2})(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i})^{2}\right]=n\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]+n(n-1)(\mathrm{E}[Y^{2}])^{2}+2n(n-1)\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{3}\right]\mathrm{E}[Y]\\
+n(n-1)(n-2)\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{2}\right](\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}.

The third term is

\mathrm{E}\left[(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i})^{4}\right]=n\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]+3n(n-1)(\mathrm{E}[Y^{2}])^{2}+4n(n-1)\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{3}\right]\mathrm{E}[Y]\\
+6n(n-1)(n-2)\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{2}\right](\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}+n(n-1)(n-2)(n-3)(\mathrm{E}[Y])^{4}.

Due to linearity of expectations and merging common terms,

\mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n-1)^{2}}\Bigg(n(n-1)^{2}\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]-4n(n-1)^{2}\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{3}\right]\mathrm{E}[Y]+n(n-1)(n^{2}-2n+3)(\mathrm{E}[Y^{2}])^{2}\\
-2n(n-1)(n-2)(n-3)\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{2}\right](\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}+n(n-1)(n-2)(n-3)(\mathrm{E}[Y])^{4}\Bigg)

(14)

Note that,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(Y-\mathrm{E}[Y])^{4}\right]=\ \mathrm{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]-4\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{3}\right]\mathrm{E}[Y]+6\left[Y^{2}\right](\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}-3\left(\mathrm{E}[Y]\right)^{4},

and

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{E}\left[(Y-\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}\right]\right)^{2}:=\sigma^{4}=\ \mathrm{E}\left[Y^{2}\right]^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[Y^{2}\right](\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}+\left(\mathrm{E}[Y]\right)^{4}.

Putting the above two expressions into (
14
) we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n^{2}(n-1)^{2}}\Bigg(n(n-1)^{2}\mathrm{E}\left[(Y-\mathrm{E}[Y])^{4}\right]+n(n-1)(n^{2}-2n+3)\left(\mathrm{E}\left[(Y-\mathrm{E}[Y])^{2}\right]\right)^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n}\mathrm{E}\left[(Y-\mathrm{E}[Y])^{4}\right]+\frac{n^{2}-2n+3}{n(n-1)}\sigma^{4}

which finishes the proof.

\halmos

10.1.18
Proof of Lemma
9.18

Proof 10.18

Proof of Lemma
9.18
.
We prove the first inequality, and the second follows similarly.

Due to Chebyshev inequality,

\displaystyle\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq\delta\right)\leq\frac{\mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1))^{2}\right]}{\delta^{2}}.

(15)

Note that,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1))^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1))^{2}\right]-\mathrm{E}[\sigma^{4}(1)].

Using Lemma
9.17
, the above can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1))^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)}\kappa(1)\sigma^{4}(1)-\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)-3}{(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1))(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)-1)}\sigma^{4}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\kappa(1)-1}{\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)}\sigma^{4}(1)+\frac{2}{(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1))(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)-1)}\sigma^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\kappa(1)-1}{\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)}\sigma^{4}(1)+\frac{3}{(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1))^{2}}\sigma^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\kappa(1)}{\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)}\sigma^{4}(1),

where the last two inequalities are due to
\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)\geq 3
.
Putting this inequality back to (
15
) we finish the proof.

\halmos

10.1.19
Proof of Lemma
9.19

Proof 10.19

Proof of Lemma
9.19
.
The proof is by applying the bounded difference inequality.

First, denote
N=\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)
as a short-hand notion.
Denote
\phi(Y_{1},...,Y_{N})=\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
to emphasize the dependence on all the potential outcomes up to
N
.
Conditional on
W_{1},...,W_{N}
, we distinguish between two cases.
If
W_{i}=0
, then

\displaystyle\phi(Y_{1},...,Y_{i},...,Y_{N})-\phi(Y_{1},...,Y^{\prime}_{i},...,Y_{N})=0.

If
W_{i}=1
, then

\displaystyle\phi(Y_{1},...,Y_{i},...,Y_{N})-\phi(Y_{1},...,Y^{\prime}_{i},...,Y_{N})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}\bigg(Y_{t}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{N}Y_{i}\bigg)^{2}+\frac{1}{N-1}\bigg(Y_{i}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{N}Y_{i}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}\bigg(Y_{t}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{N}Y^{\prime}_{i}\bigg)^{2}-\frac{1}{N-1}\bigg(Y^{\prime}_{i}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{N}Y^{\prime}_{i}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}\bigg\{\frac{2}{N}\bigg(Y_{t}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg)\left(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i}\right)+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)\bigg\}

(16)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N-1}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{N-1}{N}Y_{i}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg)^{2}-\bigg(\frac{N-1}{N}Y^{\prime}_{i}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}.

(17)

To start with (
16
), we see that it is equal to

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}\frac{2}{N}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\bigg(Y_{t}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg)+\frac{1}{N-1}\frac{N-1}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{N(N-1)}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}-\frac{N-1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg)+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{N^{2}(N-1)}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right).

Next, focusing on (
17
), we see that it is equal to

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{N-1}\bigg\{\frac{(N-1)^{2}}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)+2\frac{N-1}{N}\frac{1}{N}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}:W_{t^{\prime}}=1\\
t^{\prime}\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t^{\prime}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{N-1}{N^{2}}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)+\frac{2}{N^{2}}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}.

Combining both parts, we have

\displaystyle|\phi(Y_{1},...,Y_{i},...,Y_{N})-\phi(Y_{1},...,Y^{\prime}_{i},...,Y_{N})|=

\displaystyle\ \bigg|\frac{1}{N}\left(Y_{i}^{2}-(Y^{\prime}_{i})^{2}\right)+\frac{2}{N(N-1)}(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{N}\bigg|(Y^{\prime}_{i}-Y_{i})\bigg(\frac{2}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}-(Y^{\prime}_{i}+Y_{i})\bigg)\bigg|.

Note that for any
x,y,z\in[-V,V]
, we have

\displaystyle|(x-y)(2z-(x+y))|\leq

\displaystyle\ \max\left\{|(x-y)(2V-(x+y))|,|(x-y)(-2V-(x+y))|\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \max\left\{|2(x-y)V-(x^{2}-y^{2})|,|2(x-y)V+(x^{2}-y^{2})|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 4V^{2},

where the first inequality is because the function is monotone with respect to
z
;
the last inequality is because both functions are monotone with respect to
x
and
y
.
Replacing
x=Y^{\prime}_{i}
,
y=Y_{i}
,
z=\frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t:W_{t}=1\\
t\neq i\end{subarray}}Y_{t}
, and
V=C\sigma(1)
into the above inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\phi(Y_{1},...,Y_{i},...,Y_{N})-\phi(Y_{1},...,Y^{\prime}_{i},...,Y_{N})|\leq\frac{4C^{2}\sigma^{2}(1)}{N},

which finishes discussing the case of
W_{i}=1
.

Using the bounded difference inequality
(
Boucheron et al. 2013
,
McDiarmid et al. 1989
)
,

\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq\delta\right)\leq 2\exp\left\{-\frac{2\delta^{2}}{\sum_{t:W_{t}=1}\frac{16C^{4}\sigma^{4}(1)}{N^{2}}}\right\}\\
=2\exp\left\{-\frac{\delta^{2}N}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(1)}\right\}=2\exp\left\{-\frac{\delta^{2}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(1)\right)}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(1)}\right\}.

Similarly, we can show

\displaystyle\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)|\geq\delta\right)\leq 2\exp\left\{-\frac{\delta^{2}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{m}T_{l}(0)\right)}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(0)}\right\},

which finishes the proof.

\halmos

10.2
Derivations of Equations in Sections
2
and
3

In the main paper, we did not provide proofs to (
2
) and (
3
) because they are very well-known.
We provide proofs to (
2
) and (
3
) here.

Derivation of (
2
).

Consider the case when
T(1)
and
T(0)
are fixed.
Note that there are two sources of randomness: the treatment assignments are random, and the potential outcomes are also random.
Using the law of total variance,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widehat{\tau})=\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right)\right]+\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right]\right).

We derive both terms separately.
First,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T(1)^{2}}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t:W_{t}=1}Y_{t}(1)\right)+\frac{1}{T(0)^{2}}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t:W_{t}=0}Y_{t}(0)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T(1)^{2}}\cdot T(1)\cdot\mathrm{Var}(Y(1))+\frac{1}{T(0)^{2}}\cdot T(0)\cdot\mathrm{Var}(Y(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0).

Since the expression of
\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right)
only directly depends on
T(1)
and
T(0)
but not directly on
W_{1},...,W_{T}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right)\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0).

Second,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T(1)}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t:W_{t}=1}Y_{t}(1)\right]-\frac{1}{T(0)}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t:W_{t}=0}Y_{t}(0)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}[Y(1)]-\mathrm{E}[Y(0)].

Since the expression of
\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right]
does not depend on
W_{1},...,W_{T}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{\tau}|W_{1},...,W_{T}\right]\right)=

\displaystyle 0.

Combining both parts,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widehat{\tau})=\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0).

Derivation of (
3
).

Consider the following problem:

\displaystyle\inf_{0<x<T}

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{T-x}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{x}\sigma^{2}(0).

Consider the first order condition, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(T-x)^{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)-\frac{1}{x^{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)=0.

Simplifying terms this reduces to

\displaystyle x=\frac{\sigma^{(}0)}{\sigma(1)+\sigma(0)}T.

And the optimal objective value is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T-\frac{\sigma^{(}0)}{\sigma(1)+\sigma(0)}T}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{\frac{\sigma^{(}0)}{\sigma(1)+\sigma(0)}T}\sigma^{2}(0)=\ \frac{1}{T}\cdot(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}.

10.3
Proof of Theorem
3.1

Proof 10.20

Proof of Theorem
3.1
.
Since this is a single-stage experiment, we use
V(T(1),T(0))
instead of
\mathrm{E}[V(T(1),T(0))]
.
Suppose the optimal solution is not
T(1)=T(0)=T/2
.
Without loss of generality, assume the optimal solution is such that
T(1)>T(0)>0
.
Then for any
(T(1),T(0))
, the worst case
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
should solve the following problem,

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{V(T(1),T(0))}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}.

(18)

Using (
3
), the above expression can be re-written as

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0))}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=\frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}\cdot(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

When
\sigma(0)\neq 0
, denote
\rho=\sigma(1)/\sigma(0)\in[0,+\infty)
.
Further denote

\displaystyle g(\rho)=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}\cdot(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\rho^{2}+\frac{1}{T(0)}}{\frac{1}{T}\cdot(\rho+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T(1)}\cdot\frac{\rho^{2}}{(\rho+1)^{2}}+\frac{T}{T(0)}\cdot\frac{1}{(\rho+1)^{2}}

Taking first order derivative,

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(\rho)=

\displaystyle\frac{2T}{(\rho+1)^{3}}\cdot\left(\frac{\rho}{T(1)}-\frac{1}{T(0)}\right).

So
g(\rho)
is an increasing function when
\rho>T(1)/T(0)
, and an decreasing function when
\rho<T(1)/T(0)
.
The maximum value of
g(\rho)
is taken when either
\rho=0
or
\rho\to+\infty
.
Denote
g(+\infty)=\lim_{\rho\to+\infty}g(\rho)
.

Putting the above back to (
18
), we have, for any
(T(1),T(0))
such that
T(1)>T(0)>0
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{V(T(1),T(0))}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=\max\{g(0),g(+\infty)\}=\max\left\{\frac{T}{T(1)},\frac{T}{T(0)}\right\}>2,

where the last inequality holds because
T(0)<T/2
.
This suggests that, if
T(1)>T(0)>0
, then

\displaystyle\inf_{T(1),T(0)}\ \sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{V(T(1),T(0))}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}>2.

On the other hand, when
T(1)=T(0)=T/2
.
For any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(\frac{T}{2},\frac{T}{2})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=\frac{\frac{2}{T}\cdot(\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0))}{\frac{1}{T}\cdot(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=2\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)\ }{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\leq 2.

(19)

This suggests that

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{V(\frac{T}{2},\frac{T}{2})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=2.

Combining both cases, the optimal solution must be
T(1)=T(0)=T/2
.

To prove the second part of the Theorem, we focus on the inequality in (
19
).
The inequality holds when either
\sigma(1)=0
or
\sigma(0)=0
.

\halmos

10.4
Proof of Theorem
4.1

Proof 10.21

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.
Without loss of generality, we assume
\sigma(1)\geq\sigma(0)
throughout the proof.
Our analysis of the two-stage adaptive Neyman allocation (Algorithm
1
) will be based on the following two events.

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)\right|<2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)\right|<2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\bigg\}.

Denote
\mathcal{E}=\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)
. Then
\Pr(\mathcal{E})=\Pr(\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))\geq 1-\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(1))-\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(0))
.
We further have

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\ 1-\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq 2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\right)-\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)|\geq 2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 1-\frac{\kappa(1)\sigma^{4}(1)}{2T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\sigma^{4}(1)T_{1}(1)}-\frac{\kappa(0)\sigma^{4}(0)}{2T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\sigma^{4}(0)T_{1}(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-\frac{\kappa(1)+\kappa(0)}{T^{\varepsilon}},

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.18
.

Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(1)\left(1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(1)\left(1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right),

(20a)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(0)\left(1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(0)\left(1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right).

(20b)

Due to (
20a
) and (
20b
), and given that
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we have
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)>0
.
Denote
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
and
\widehat{\rho}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}
.

Now we distinguish two cases, and discuss these two cases separately.

1.

Case 1
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

2.

Case 2
:

\displaystyle\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Note that, for case 2, we do not discuss
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, because we assume that
\sigma(1)\geq\sigma(0)
.
For each of the above two cases, we further discuss two sub-cases.
The remaining of the proof is structured as enumerating all four cases.
After enumerating all four sub-cases we finish the proof.

Case 1.1
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 3 instead of Line 5 or Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by (
6
).
We re-write (
6
) again as follows,

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T).

With a little abuse of notation, we write
V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})
to stand for
V(T(1),T(0))
, where we emphasize that this is a random quantity (as
T(1)
and
T(0)
are random) that is conditional on event
\mathcal{E}
.
Putting
(T(1),T(0))
into (
4
), we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\left(1+\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}\right)\sigma^{2}(1)+\left(1+\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}\right)\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{1}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)-2\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\right)

(21)

Due to Lemma
9.2
, and using (
20a
) and (
20b
),

\frac{1}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)-2\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\right)\\
\leq\ \frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}-2\right).

(22)

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(23)

Note also that

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}-2=

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}}}-2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 2\left(1-2T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}-2

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\left(1+2T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\right)-2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 4T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon},

(24)

where the inequality holds when
T^{\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon}\geq 2
.
This is because
T\geq 16
and
\varepsilon\in(0,\frac{1}{8})
, so we have
T^{\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon}\geq T^{\frac{1}{4}}\geq 2
.

Combining (
21
) — (
24
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}.

Case 1.2
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

but

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ \text{or}\ \widehat{\rho}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

If
\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, then

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by
(T(1),T(0))=(T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}},\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}})
.

Note that,

\displaystyle\rho\ =\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\ \leq\ \frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ <\ \widehat{\rho}\ \leq\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}.

Putting
(T(1),T(0))
into (
4
), we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\sigma(0)}{\sigma(1)}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}-2\right).

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
.
Combining this with (
23
) and (
24
) we have again

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}.

If
\widehat{\rho}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, then Algorithm
1
goes to Line 5.

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\ <\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ \leq\ \rho\ =\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)},

and the same analysis follows similarly.

Case 2.1
:

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by
(T(1),T(0))=(T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}},\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}})
.

Putting
(T(1),T(0))
into (
4
), we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\rho^{2}}{(\rho+1)^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{(\rho+1)^{2}}.

(25)

Due to Lemma
9.1
, since
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we know that the expression in (
25
) is increasing with respect to
\rho
.
So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\lim_{\rho\to+\infty}\left(\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\rho^{2}}{(\rho+1)^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{(\rho+1)^{2}}\right)=\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1+T^{-\frac{1}{2}},

where the last inequality holds because
T\geq 1
.

Case 2.2
:

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}

Note that,

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)\geq

\displaystyle\ \sigma(1)\sqrt{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ \sigma(0)\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sqrt{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

where the first inequality is due to (
20a
);
the second inequality is due to
\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
;
the third inequality is due to (
20b
);
the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.3
.

The above shows that, in this case (Case 2.2),

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\frac{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 3 instead of Line 5 or Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by (
6
), which we write again as follows,

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T).

Similar to Case 1.1, combining (
21
) — (
24
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}.

To conclude, in all four cases,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+T^{-\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}.

\halmos

10.5
Proof of Theorem
5.1

Proof 10.22

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.
We first show Algorithm
2
is feasible.
To start, it is easy to see
1<\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}
.
Then for any
m\leq M-2
,

\displaystyle\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}=6\cdot 15^{-\frac{m}{M}}\cdot T^{\frac{m}{M}}<6\cdot 15^{-\frac{m+1}{M}}\cdot T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}=\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}},

where the inequality is because
T>15
.
Finally,

\displaystyle\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}=6\cdot 15^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\cdot T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\leq 6\cdot 15^{-\frac{2}{3}}\cdot T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\approx 0.9866\cdot T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}<T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\leq T,

where the first inequality is because
M\geq 3
.
Combining all above we know Algorithm
2
is feasible, i.e.,
1<\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}<...<\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}<T
.

Then we analyze the performance of Algorithm
2
.
Our analysis of Algorithm
2
relies on a clean event analysis, which has been widely used in the online learning literature to prove upper bounds
(
Badanidiyuru et al. 2018
,
Lattimore and
Szepesvári 2020
,
Slivkins 2019
)
, and has been recently used in the stochastic control literature to prove lower bounds
(
Arlotto and Gurvich 2019
)
.

To proceed with the clean event analysis, suppose there are two length-
T
arrays for both the treated and the control, with each value being an independent and identically distributed copy of the representative random variables
Y(1)
and
Y(0)
, respectively.
When Algorithm
2
suggests to conduct an
m
-th stage experiment parameterized by
(T_{m}(1),T_{m}(0))
, the observations from the
m
-th stage experiment are generated by reading the next
T_{m}(1)
values from the treated array, and the next
T_{m}(0)
values from the control array.
See Figure
10.22
for an illustration.

\TABLE

Illustration of the clear event analysis

estimates
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)

\overbrace{\hskip 187.78818pt}

Treated

Z_{1}(1)

Z_{2}(1)

\ldots

Z_{s}(1)

\ldots

Z_{s^{\prime}}(1)

\ldots

Z_{T}(1)

Control

Z_{1}(0)

Z_{2}(0)

\ldots

Z_{s}(0)

\ldots

Z_{s^{\prime}}(0)

\ldots

Z_{T}(0)

\underbrace{\hskip 284.52756pt}

estimates
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m^{\prime}}(0)

Note: In this illustration, the treated array contains random values
Z_{1}(1)
,
Z_{2}(1)
, …,
Z_{T}(1)
and the control array contains random values
Z_{1}(0)
,
Z_{2}(0)
, …,
Z_{T}(0)
. In this illustration, we use the first
s=\frac{\beta_{m}}{2}T^{\frac{m}{M}}
values in the treated array to compute the sample variance estimator
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
, and the first
s^{\prime}=\frac{\beta_{m^{\prime}}}{2}T^{\frac{m^{\prime}}{M}}
values in the control array to compute the sample variance estimator
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m^{\prime}}(0)
. In this table, all the sample variance estimators such as
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m^{\prime}}(0)
are all well-defined under a
fixed
number of values.

Even though Algorithm
2
adaptively determines the number of treated and control subjects, it is always the first few values of of the two arrays that are read.
For any
m\leq M-1
, let
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)
be the sample variance estimators obtained from reading the first
\frac{\beta_{m}}{2}T^{\frac{m}{M}}
values in the treated array and control array, respectively.
Depending on the execution of Algorithm
2
, only a few of the sample variance estimators
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
or
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)
are calculated.
When one sample variance estimator
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
is calculated following Algorithm
2
, it is equivalent to reading the corresponding
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
from Table
10.22
.

Define the following events.
For any
m\leq M-1
, define

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{m}(1)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)\right|<2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)\right|<2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\bigg\}.

Denote the intersect of all above events as
\mathcal{E}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}=\bigcap_{m=1}^{M-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{m}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)\right).

Then due to union bound,

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})\geq 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{m}(1))-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{m}(0)).

We further have

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr\left(|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq 2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\right)-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr\left(|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)|\geq 2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\frac{\kappa(1)\sigma^{4}(1)}{2\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}+\varepsilon}\sigma^{4}(1)\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\frac{\kappa(0)\sigma^{4}(0)}{2\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}+\varepsilon}\sigma^{4}(0)\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\frac{\kappa(1)+\kappa(0)}{T^{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-(M-1)\frac{\kappa(1)+\kappa(0)}{T^{\varepsilon}},

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.18
.

Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have, for any
m\leq M-1
,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(1)\left(1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(1)\left(1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right),

(26a)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(0)\left(1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(0)\left(1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}\right).

(26b)

Since
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we can denote
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
.
For any
m\leq M-1
, when
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)
are calculated during Algorithm
2
,
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)=\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)=\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)
.
Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, due to (
26a
) and (
26b
), and given that
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we have
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)>0
.
Then we can denote
\widehat{\rho}_{m}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}
.

In the remaining of the analysis, we distinguish several cases and discuss these cases separately.
Recall that
\widehat{\rho}_{1}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}
.
Without loss of generality, assume

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\geq 1.

(27)

Case 1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

Case 1.1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
5
in the
1
-st stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}\bigg).

With a little abuse of notation, we write
V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})
to stand for
V(T(1),T(0))
, where we emphasize that this is a random quantity (as
T(1)
and
T(0)
are random) that is conditional on event
\mathcal{E}
.
We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(28)

Recall that
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
.
We further distinguish two cases.

First
, if
\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
, then we write (
28
) as

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\displaystyle\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}.

(29)

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot 4\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon},

(30)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
and (
29
); the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
29
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
30
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+4T^{-1+\varepsilon}<1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon},

where the last inequality is because
T>15
so
T^{-1+\varepsilon}=T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}<15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}
.

Second
, if
\rho\geq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
, then we write (
28
) as

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}=1+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}<1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.1
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.7
.

Combining
\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
and
\rho\geq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
we have that in Case 1.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case 1.2
:

\displaystyle\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}=\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}+1}T\leq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
8
in the
1
-st stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{1}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(31)

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (
26a
) and (
26b
) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot 4\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon},

(32)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
26a
) and (
26b
); the second inequality is due to the condition of Case 1.2; the third inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
32
) we have that in Case 1.2,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+\frac{4\beta_{2}}{\beta_{1}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}=\ 1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case 2
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

Due to (
27
) we know that
\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)\geq\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)
.
In Case 2 we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
12
in the 1-st stage experiment.
We further distinguish two cases.

Case 2.1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}},

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{2}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
5
in the
2
-nd stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}\bigg).

We can express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\\
<\widehat{\rho}_{2}\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}},

(33)

where the first and the fourth inequalities are due to (
26a
) and (
26b
); the second and the third inequalities are due to the condition of Case 2.1;
the last inequality is because
\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}<\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}
so we have
2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}<2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\left(\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\right)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(4\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}+\varepsilon}\right),

(34)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
33
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
34
) we have that in Case 2.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+\frac{4\beta_{2}}{\beta_{1}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}=1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case 2.2
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}},

\displaystyle\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{2}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}=\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T\leq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}+1}T<\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
8
in the
2
-nd stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{2}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(35)

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (

) and (

) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{2}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{2}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot 4\beta_{2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{2}{M}+\varepsilon},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma

; the last inequality is due to Lemma

Note that,

is a decreasing function when

.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}>1,

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{T}

Putting this into (

Case
\bm{m}
(when

):

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{l}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}},\ \forall\ l\leq m-1.

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}\geq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\geq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\\
\geq\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{4}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}},

) and (

); the third inequality is due to (

); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have
\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
12
in the (m-1)-th stage experiment.
We further distinguish two cases.

Case
\bm{m}
.1
:
In addition to the conditions in Case

above, we also have

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{m}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}.

Similar to the analysis in Case 2.1, we proceed with the following analysis.
In Case

.1,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

in the

Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}\bigg).

We can express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}\\
<\widehat{\rho}_{m}\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}},

(37)

where the first and the fourth inequalities are due to (

) and (

);

.1;
the last inequality is because

so we have

.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\left(\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\right)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

Putting this into (

) we have that in Case

.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+\frac{4\beta_{m}}{\beta_{m-1}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}=1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case
\bm{m}
.2
:
In addition to the conditions in Case

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}=\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}+1}T\leq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}+1}T<\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}.

in the

Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{m}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{m}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (

) and (
