So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot 4\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}+\varepsilon},

(39)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{m}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
26a
) and (
26b
); the second inequality is due to the condition of Case 2.2; the third inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\frac{\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\frac{\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{T}

Putting this into (
39
) we have that in Case
m
.2,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+\frac{4\beta_{m+1}}{\beta_{m}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}=\ 1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case (
\bm{M-1}
)
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{l}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}},\ \forall\ l\leq M-2.

Due to the condition of Case (
M-1
), we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\widehat{\rho}_{M-2}\geq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\geq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\\
\geq\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{4}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}},

where the first and second inequalities are due to (
26a
) and (
26b
); the third inequality is due to (
27
); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have
\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{M-2}}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
12
in the
(M-2)
-th stage experiment.
Then Algorithm
2
goes to Line
21
in the last stage.
We further distinguish two cases.

Case (
\bm{M-1}
).1
:
In addition to the conditions in Case
(M-1)
above, we also have

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}.

Similar to the analysis in Case
m
.1, we proceed with the following analysis.
In Case
(M-1)
.1,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
24
in the
(M-1)
-th stage experiment, and we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\bigg).

We can express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{M-2}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\\
<\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}},

(40)

where the first and the fourth inequalities are due to (
26a
) and (
26b
);
the second and the third inequalities are due to the conditions of Case
(M-1)
.1;
the last inequality is because
\beta_{M-2}T^{\frac{M-2}{M}}<\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}
so we have
2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}<2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\left(\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\right)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{M-2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-2}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(4\beta_{M-2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-2}{M}+\varepsilon}\right),

(41)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
40
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\frac{\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\frac{\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
41
) we have that in Case
(M-1)
.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+\frac{4\beta_{M-1}}{\beta_{M-2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}=1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{M}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

Case (
\bm{M-1}
).2
:
In addition to the conditions in Case
(M-1)
above, we also have

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}.

Due to the condition of Case (
M-1
).2, we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\geq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\geq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\\
\geq\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{4}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}},

where the first and second inequalities are due to (
26a
) and (
26b
); the third inequality is due to (
27
); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma
9.5
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.6
.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have
\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
26
in the
(M-1)
-th stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (
26a
) and (
26b
) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}{1-2^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-2\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot 4\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.4
; the last inequality is because
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.

Finally, using the definition of
\beta_{M-1}=6\cdot 15^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}
,

\displaystyle\beta_{M-1}^{-1}=\frac{1}{6}\cdot 15^{\frac{M-1}{M}}=\frac{15}{6}\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{15}}<4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{15}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}<1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{15}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

To conclude, in all cases, we have shown that

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+4\cdot 15^{-\frac{1}{15}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}+\varepsilon}.

\halmos

10.6
Proof of Theorem
5.3

Proof 10.23

Proof of Theorem
5.3
.
Fix any adaptive design of experiment
\eta
.
Let
T\geq 4
and define

\displaystyle\epsilon=\frac{1}{3T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\frac{1}{6}.

Let there be two discrete probability distributions
\nu
and
\nu^{\prime}
, defined as follows.
Both distributions have three discrete supports
\{-1,0,1\}
.
The probability mass for distribution
\nu
is given by

\displaystyle p_{-1}=\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle p_{0}=\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle p_{1}=\frac{1}{3}.

The probability mass for distribution
\nu^{\prime}
is given by

\displaystyle p^{\prime}_{-1}=\frac{1}{3}+\frac{\epsilon}{2},

\displaystyle p^{\prime}_{0}=\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon,

\displaystyle p^{\prime}_{1}=\frac{1}{3}+\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

Then we immediately have

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(\nu)=\frac{2}{3},

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(\nu^{\prime})=\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon.

Moreover, we upper bound the KL-divergences of these two probability distributions as follows.

\displaystyle D_{KL}(\nu||\nu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\right)+\frac{1}{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{1-3\epsilon}\right)+\frac{1}{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{1-\frac{27}{4}\epsilon^{2}-\frac{27}{4}\epsilon^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\log\left(1+\frac{27}{2}\epsilon^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\cdot\frac{27}{2}\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2},

(42)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.9
;
the second inequality is because for any
x>0
,
\log{(1+x)}\leq x
.

On the other hand, the KL-divergence calculated in the other way is upper bounded by.

\displaystyle D_{KL}(\nu^{\prime}||\nu)=

\displaystyle\ \left(\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon\right)\log\left(1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon\right)+\left(\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon\right)\log\left(1-3\epsilon\right)+\left(\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon\right)\log\left(1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \left(\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon\right)\log\left(1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon\right)+\left(\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon\right)\log\left(1-3\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \left(\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon\right)\left(\frac{3}{2}\epsilon\right)+\left(\frac{1}{3}-\epsilon\right)\left(-3\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \epsilon+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon^{2}-\epsilon+3\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2},

(43)

where the first inequality is because first, for any
x>0
,
\log{(1+x)}\leq x
, and second, for any
0<x<1
,
\log{(1-x)}\leq-x
.

We will use these two probability distributions to construct two problem instances.
Consider the first problem instance where
Y(1)\sim\nu^{\prime},Y(0)\sim\nu
.
Denote
\Pr_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}
as the probability distribution induced by this problem instance and by the design of experiment
\eta
, where we drop the dependence on
\eta
as it is clear from the context.
Denote
\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}
as the expectation taken under
\Pr_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}
.

Similarly, consider the second problem instance where
Y(1)\sim\nu,Y(0)\sim\nu^{\prime}
.
Denote
\Pr_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}
as the probability distribution induced by this problem instance and by the design of experiment
\eta
, where we drop the dependence on
\eta
.
Denote
\mathrm{E}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}
as the expectation taken under
\Pr_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}
.

Now we focus on the first instance
(Y(1),Y(0))\sim(\nu^{\prime},\nu)
.
Note that
\sigma^{2}(1)=\sigma^{2}(\nu^{\prime})>\sigma^{2}(\nu)=\sigma^{2}(0)
.
When
T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{T^{\eta}(1)}{T^{\eta}(0)}\leq 1<\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}.

Due to Lemma
9.2
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{T^{\eta}(0)}{T^{\eta}(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T^{\eta}(1)}{T^{\eta}(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\geq\frac{2\sigma^{2}(1)+2\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

On the other hand, when
T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}
, the ratio is greater or equal to
1
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}\geq 1.

Putting the above two cases together we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}\geq

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left(T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{2\cdot(\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon)+2\cdot(\frac{2}{3})}{\left(\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon}+\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\right)^{2}}+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left(T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot 1.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left(T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon-\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon+\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}.

(44)

We further have

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon-\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon+\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}\geq\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon-\frac{4}{3}(1+\frac{3}{4}\epsilon-\frac{9}{64}\epsilon^{2})}{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon+\frac{4}{3}(1+\frac{3}{4}\epsilon)}=\frac{\frac{3}{16}\epsilon^{2}}{\frac{8}{3}+2\epsilon}\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.8
; the last inequality is due to
\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{6}
.
Putting the above inequality into (
44
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}>1+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left(T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16}.

(45)

Next we focus on the second instance
(Y(1),Y(0))\sim(\nu,\nu^{\prime})
.
Similar to the above analysis, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}\geq

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\left(T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot 1+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\left(T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{2\cdot(\frac{2}{3})+2\cdot(\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon)}{\left(\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}+\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}+\epsilon}\right)^{2}}.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\left(T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon-\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}{\frac{4}{3}+\epsilon+\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{1+\frac{3}{2}\epsilon}}.

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ 1+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\left(T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}\right)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16}.

(46)

Combining (
45
) and (
46
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}+

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{E}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 2+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16}\cdot\left(\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left(T^{\eta}(1)\leq\frac{T}{2}\right)+\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\left(T^{\eta}(1)>\frac{T}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 2+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16}\cdot\exp\left\{-D_{KL}\left(\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu},\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\right)\right\},

(47)

where the second inequality is due to Bretagnolle-Huber inequality
(
Bretagnolle and
Huber 1979
,
Lattimore and
Szepesvári 2020
)
.

Next we upper bound
D_{KL}\left(\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu},\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}}\right)
.

\displaystyle D_{KL}(\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}||\mathrm{Pr}_{\nu,\nu^{\prime}})=

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[T^{\eta}(1)\right]D_{KL}(\nu^{\prime}||\nu)+\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[T^{\eta}(0)\right]D_{KL}(\nu||\nu^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[T^{\eta}(1)\right]\frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2}+\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[T^{\eta}(0)\right]\frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T\cdot\frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2},

where the inequality is due to (
42
) and (
43
).
Putting this into (
47
) we have

\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}+\frac{\mathrm{E}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}\geq 2+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{16}\exp\left\{-T\cdot\frac{9}{2}\epsilon^{2}\right\}\\
=2+\frac{1}{144\exp{\left\{\frac{1}{2}\right\}}}\cdot T^{-1}\geq 2+\frac{1}{240}T^{-1},

where the equality is using
\epsilon=\frac{1}{3T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.
Using the above inequality we have

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{\mathrm{E}_{\nu^{\prime},\nu}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)},\frac{\mathrm{E}\left[V(T^{\eta}(1),T^{\eta}(0))\right]}{V\left(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0)\right)}\right\}\geq 1+\frac{1}{480}\cdot T^{-1}.

\halmos

10.7
Proof of Corollary
6.1

10.7.1
Establishing a high probability bound.

We first establish a high probability bound for the proof of Corollary
6.1
.

Lemma 10.24

Let
T\geq 320^{\frac{5}{4}}C^{5}
.
Let
\beta=4C^{2}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}
in Algorithm
1
.
Let
(T(1),T(0))
be the number of total treated and control subjects from Algorithm
1
, respectively. Under Assumption
6
, there exists an event that happens with probability at least
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
, conditional on which

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{\mathrm{E}[V(T(1),T(0))]}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof 10.25

Proof of Lemma
10.24
.
Without loss of generality, we assume
\sigma(1)\geq\sigma(0)
throughout the proof.
We consider the following two events.

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)\right|<2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma^{2}(1)\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)\right|<2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma^{2}(0)\bigg\}.

Denote
\mathcal{E}=\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)
. Then
\Pr(\mathcal{E})=\Pr(\mathcal{E}_{1}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}(0))\geq 1-\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(1))-\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(0))
.
We further have

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\ 1-\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq 2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma^{2}(1)\right)-\Pr\left(|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)|\geq 2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma^{2}(0)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 1-2\exp\left\{-\frac{4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{4}(1)\cdot T_{1}(1)}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(1)}\right\}-2\exp\left\{-\frac{4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{4}(0)\cdot T_{1}(0)}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(0)}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-4T^{-2},

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.19
;
and the last equality is using
T_{1}(1)=T_{1}(0)=4C^{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}

Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(1)\left(1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(1)\left(1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\right),

(48a)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(0)\left(1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(0)\left(1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}\right).

(48b)

Due to (
48a
) and (
48b
), and given that
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we have
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(1),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{1}(0)>0
.
Denote
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
and
\widehat{\rho}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}
.

Now we distinguish two cases, and discuss these two cases separately.

1.

Case 1
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

2.

Case 2
:

\displaystyle\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Note that, for case 2, we do not discuss
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, because we assume that
\sigma(1)\geq\sigma(0)
.
For each of the above two cases, we further discuss two sub-cases.
The remaining of the proof is structured as enumerating all four cases.
After enumerating all four sub-cases we finish the proof.

Case 1.1
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 3 instead of Line 5 or Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by (
6
).
We re-write (
6
) again as follows,

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T).

Putting
(T(1),T(0))
into (
4
), we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\left(1+\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}\right)\sigma^{2}(1)+\left(1+\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}\right)\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{1}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)-2\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\right)

(49)

Due to Lemma
9.2
, and using (
48a
) and (
48b
),

\frac{1}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}}\ \sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}\ \sigma^{2}(1)-2\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\right)\\
\leq\ \frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}-2\right).

(50)

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(51)

Note also that

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}-2=

\displaystyle\ \frac{2}{\sqrt{1-4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}-2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 2\left(1-4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}-2

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 2\left(1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)-2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 8C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}},

(52)

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.12
-(iii).

Combining (
49
) — (
52
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+2C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Case 1.2
:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\rho\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

but

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ \text{or}\ \widehat{\rho}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

If
\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, then

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by
(T(1),T(0))=(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}},\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}})
.

Note that,

\displaystyle\rho\ =\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\ \leq\ \frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ <\ \widehat{\rho}\ \leq\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}.

Then we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\sigma(0)}{\sigma(1)}\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}-2\right).

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
.
Combining this with (
51
) and (
52
) we have again

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+2C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

If
\widehat{\rho}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, then Algorithm
1
goes to Line 5.

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\ <\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ \leq\ \rho\ =\ \frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)},

and the same analysis follows similarly.

Case 2.1
:

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\widehat{\rho}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by
(T(1),T(0))=(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}},\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}})
.

Putting
(T(1),T(0))
into (
4
), we have, for any
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T(1)}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{T(0)}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\rho^{2}}{(\rho+1)^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{(\rho+1)^{2}}.

(53)

Due to Lemma
9.1
, since
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we know that the expression in (
53
) is increasing with respect to
\rho
.
So we have

\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\lim_{\rho\to+\infty}\left(\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\rho^{2}}{(\rho+1)^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{(\rho+1)^{2}}\right)\\
=\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}=\frac{T}{T-2C^{2}T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}},

where the last inequality holds because
T\geq 64C^{4}\log{T}>16C^{4}\log{T}
.

Case 2.2
:

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}

Note that,

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)\geq

\displaystyle\ \sigma(1)\sqrt{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ \sigma(0)\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sqrt{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{1+2CT^{-\frac{1}{4}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{4}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

where the first inequality is due to (
48a
);
the second inequality is due to
\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
;
the third inequality is due to (
48b
);
the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.12
-(ii).

The above shows that, in this case (Case 2.2),

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1}\ T=\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Due to this, Algorithm
1
goes to Line 3 instead of Line 5 or Line 7.
The total numbers of treated and control subjects are given by (
6
), which we write again as follows,

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T).

Similar to Case 1.1, combining (
49
) — (
52
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+2C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

To conclude, in all four cases,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

\halmos

10.7.2
Completing the proof of Corollary
6.1
.

Proof 10.26

Proof of Corollary
6.1
.
We first show Algorithm
1
is feasible under
\beta=4C^{2}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
This is because

\displaystyle\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\cdot 8C^{2}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot T^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot T^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot T^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{2}T,

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.10
.

Next, due to Lemma
10.24
, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
that happens with probability at least
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(54)

On the other hand, on the low probability event
\overline{\mathcal{E}}
that happens with probability at most
\frac{4}{T^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\overline{\mathcal{E}})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \max\left\{\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}},\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta T^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 2\beta^{-1}T^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 2^{-1}C^{-2}(\log{T})^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}},

(55)

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.1
.

So overall we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{\mathrm{E}[V(T(1),T(0))]}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \left(1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}\right)\left(1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\frac{4}{T^{2}}\cdot 2^{-1}C^{-2}(\log{T})^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{2}{T}\cdot C^{-2}(\log{T})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot T^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+4C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\cdot C^{2}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot T^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+5C^{2}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}},

where the first inequality is using the total law of probability, and upper bounding the two parts using (
54
) and (
55
);
the second probability is upper bounding
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
by 1;
the third inequality is because
T\geq 2
and
C^{4}\log{T}\geq 1
.

\halmos

10.8
Proof of Corollary
6.2

10.8.1
Establishing a high probability bound.

We first establish a high probability bound for the proof of Corollary
6.2
.

Lemma 10.27

Let
M\geq 3
and
T\geq(\frac{5000}{3})^{\frac{5}{4}}C^{5}
.
Let the tuning parameters from Algorithm
2
be defined as
\beta_{m}=\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot(\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T})^{-\frac{m}{M}}
.
Let
(T(1),T(0))
be the total number of treated and control subjects from Algorithm
2
, respectively.
Under Assumption
4
, there exists an event that happens with probability at least
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
, conditional on which

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{\mathrm{E}[V(T(1),T(0))]}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Proof 10.28

Proof of Lemma
10.27
.
We proceed with the similar clean event analysis as in Theorem
5.1
.
Suppose there are two length-
T
arrays for both the treated and the control, with each value being an independent and identically distributed copy of the representative random variables
Y(1)
and
Y(0)
, respectively.
When Algorithm
2
suggests to conduct an
m
-th stage experiment parameterized by
(T_{m}(1),T_{m}(0))
, the observations from the
m
-th stage experiment are generated by reading the next
T_{m}(1)
values from the treated array, and the next
T_{m}(0)
values from the control array.

Even though Algorithm
2
adaptively determines the number of treated and control subjects, it is always the first few values of of the two arrays that are read.
For any
m\leq M-1
, let
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)
be the sample variance estimators obtained from reading the first
\frac{\beta_{m}}{2}T^{\frac{m}{M}}
values in the treated array and control array, respectively.
Depending on the execution of Algorithm
2
, only a few of the sample variance estimators
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
or
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)
are calculated.
When one sample variance estimator
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
is calculated following Algorithm
2
, it is equivalent to reading the corresponding
\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
from the array.

Define the following events.
For any
m\leq M-1
, define

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{m}(1)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)\right|<48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)=

\displaystyle\ \bigg\{\left|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)\right|<48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\bigg\}.

Denote the intersect of all above events as
\mathcal{E}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}=\bigcap_{m=1}^{M-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{m}(1)\cap\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)\right).

Then due to union bound,

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})\geq 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{m}(1))-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr(\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{m}(0)).

We further have

\displaystyle\Pr(\mathcal{E})=

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr\left(|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)-\sigma^{2}(1)|\geq 48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\Pr\left(|\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)-\sigma^{2}(0)|\geq 48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{2}(0)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}2\exp\left\{-\frac{48C^{4}\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}}\log{T}\sigma^{4}(1)\cdot\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(1)}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}2\exp\left\{-\frac{48C^{4}\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}}\log{T}\sigma^{4}(0)\cdot\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{8C^{4}\sigma^{4}(0)}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}4\exp\{-3\log{T}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1-(M-1)\frac{4}{T^{3}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ 1-\frac{4}{T^{2}},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.19
.

Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have, for any
m\leq M-1
,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(1)\left(1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(1)\left(1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(56a)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(0)\left(1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\ \leq\ \widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)\ \leq\ \sigma^{2}(0)\left(1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

(56b)

Since
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we can denote
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
.
For any
m\leq M-1
, when
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)
are calculated during Algorithm
2
,
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1)=\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(1)
and
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)=\widehat{\psi}^{2}_{m}(0)
.
Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
, due to (
56a
) and (
56b
), and given that
\sigma(1),\sigma(0)>0
, we have
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(1),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{m}(0)>0
.
Then we can denote
\widehat{\rho}_{m}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}
.

In the remaining of the analysis, we distinguish several cases and discuss these cases separately.
Recall that
\widehat{\rho}_{1}=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}
.
Without loss of generality, assume

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\geq 1.

(57)

Case 1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

Case 1.1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
5
in the
1
-st stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{\frac{1}{T}(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(58)

Recall that
\rho=\frac{\sigma(1)}{\sigma(0)}
.
We further distinguish two cases.

First
, if
\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
, then we write (
58
) as

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\displaystyle\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}.

(59)

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}\right),

(60)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
and (
59
); the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
59
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
60
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\ 1+96C^{4}T^{-1}\log{T}<1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}},

where the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.11
.

Second
, if
\rho\geq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
, then we write (
58
) as

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

So we have

\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}=1+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\\
<1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.1
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.16
.

Combining
\rho<\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
and
\rho\geq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}
, we have that in Case 1.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case 1.2
:

\displaystyle\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}=\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}+1}T\leq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
8
in the
1
-st stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{1}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(61)

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (
56a
) and (
56b
) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}\right),

(62)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\\
>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
56a
) and (
56b
); the second inequality is due to the condition of Case 1.2; the third inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
62
) we have that in Case 1.2,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{2}}{\beta_{1}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case 2
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

Due to (
27
) we know that
\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)\geq\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)
.
In Case 2 we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
12
in the 1-st stage experiment.
We further distinguish two cases.

Case 2.1
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}},

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{2}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
5
in the
2
-nd stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}},\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}\bigg).

We can express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{2}\\
\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}},

(63)

where the first and the fourth inequalities are due to (
56a
) and (
56b
); the second and the third inequalities are due to the condition of Case 2.1;
the last inequality is because
\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}<\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}
so we have
48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}<48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\left(\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{M}}\log{T}\right),

(64)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
63
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
64
) we have that in Case 2.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{2}}{\beta_{1}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case 2.2
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}},

\displaystyle\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{2}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}.

In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}=\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{2}T^{\frac{2}{M}}}+1}T\leq\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}+1}T<\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
8
in the
2
-nd stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{2}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{2}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

(65)

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (

) and (

) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{2}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{2}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{2}^{-1}T^{-\frac{2}{M}}\log{T}\right),

(66)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\\
>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (

) and (

); the second inequality is due to the condition of Case 2.2; the third inequality is due to Lemma

; the last inequality is due to Lemma

.
Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\ \frac{\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}})^{2}}\leq\ \frac{\beta_{3}T^{\frac{3}{M}}}{T}

Putting this into (
66
) we have that in Case 2.2,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{3}}{\beta_{2}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case
\bm{m}
(when
m\leq M-2
):

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{l}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}},\ \forall\ l\leq m-1.

Due to the condition of Case
m
, we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}\geq

\displaystyle\ \rho\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}},

where the first and second inequalities are due to (
56a
) and (
56b
); the third inequality is due to (
57
); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have
\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
12
in the (m-1)-th stage experiment.
We further distinguish two cases.

Case
\bm{m}
.1
:
In addition to the conditions in Case
m
above, we also have

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{m}>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}.

Similar to the analysis in Case 2.1, we proceed with the following analysis.
In Case
m
.1,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T<\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}.

goes to Line

in the

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{m-1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{m}\\
\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}},

where the first and the fourth inequalities are due to (

) and (

);

.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\left(\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{m-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m-1}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{m-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m-1}{M}}\log{T}\right),

Putting this into (
68
) we have that in Case

.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{m}}{\beta_{m-1}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case
\bm{m}
.2
:

goes to Line

in the

-th stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{m}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{m}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{m}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (

) and (
56b
) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{m}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{m}^{-1}T^{-\frac{m}{M}}\log{T}\right),

(69)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
.

Note that,
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\rho\geq\widehat{\rho}_{m}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\\
>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{m}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{m}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
56a
) and (
56b
); the second inequality is due to the condition of Case 2.2; the third inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\frac{\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\frac{\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}}}{T}

Putting this into (
69
) we have that in Case
m
.2,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{m+1}}{\beta_{m}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case (
\bm{M-1}
)
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{l}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{l+1}T^{\frac{l+1}{M}}},\ \forall\ l\leq M-2.

Due to the condition of Case (
M-1
), we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{M-2}\geq

\displaystyle\ \rho\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}},

where the first and second inequalities are due to (

); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma

; the last inequality is due to Lemma

.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have

, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-2}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

So Algorithm

goes to Line

in the
(M-2)
-th stage experiment.
Then Algorithm

goes to Line

We can express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Note that,

\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{M-2}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}<\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\\
\leq\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}<\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-2}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}},

\displaystyle\leq

(71)

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
