\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}=\frac{\rho}{(\rho+1)^{2}}
is a decreasing function when
\rho>1
.
Note also that,

\displaystyle\rho>\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}>\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}>1,

where the first inequality is due to (
70
); the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}<\frac{\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}\bigg)^{2}}=\frac{\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}(T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}})}{(T+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}})^{2}}\leq\frac{\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T}.

Putting this into (
71
) we have that in Case
(M-1)
.1,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+\frac{96C^{4}\beta_{M-1}}{\beta_{M-2}}\cdot T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=1+96\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

Case (
\bm{M-1}
).2
:
In addition to the conditions in Case
(M-1)
above, we also have

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}.

Due to the condition of Case (
M-1
).2, we immediately have

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}+1}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\geq

\displaystyle\ \rho\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \widehat{\rho}_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}},

where the first and second inequalities are due to (
56a
) and (
56b
); the third inequality is due to (
57
); the fourth inequality is due to Lemma
9.14
; the last inequality is due to Lemma
9.15
.
Due to the above sequence of inequalities, we have
\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}}\leq\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\geq\frac{1}{1+\frac{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}}}T=\frac{1}{2}\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

So Algorithm
2
goes to Line
26
in the
(M-1)
-th stage experiment.
Then we have

\displaystyle(T(1),T(0))=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T,\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}{\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(1)+\widehat{\sigma}_{M-1}(0)}T\bigg).

We can then express

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}}\sigma^{2}(1)+\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}.

Recall that, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, (
56a
) and (
56b
) lead to

\displaystyle\rho\cdot\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\leq\widehat{\rho}_{M-1}\leq\rho\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}.

So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sigma^{2}(1)+\sigma^{2}(0)+\sigma(1)\sigma(0)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{1-48^{\frac{1}{2}}C^{2}\beta_{M-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{2M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\bigg)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-48C^{4}\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}}}-2\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\cdot\left(96C^{4}\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+24C^{4}\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T},

where the first inequality is due to Lemma
9.2
; the second inequality is due to Lemma
9.13
; the last inequality is because
\frac{\sigma(1)\sigma(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.

Finally, using the definition of
\beta_{M-1}=\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot(\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T})^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}
,

24C^{4}\beta_{M-1}^{-1}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\log{T}=\frac{9}{50}\cdot\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}\\
=\frac{9}{50}\cdot\frac{1000}{3}\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\\
<96\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}},

where the last inequality is because
\frac{9}{50}\cdot\frac{1000}{3}=60<96
.
So we have

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

To conclude, in all cases, we have shown that

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

\halmos

10.8.2
Completing the proof of Corollary
6.2
.

Proof 10.29

Proof of Corollary
6.2
.
We first show Algorithm
2
is feasible under the parameters as defined in Corollary
6.2
.
To start, it is easy to see
1<\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}
.
Then for any
m\leq M-2
,

\displaystyle\beta_{m}T^{\frac{m}{M}}=\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{\frac{m}{M}}<\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{\frac{m+1}{M}}=\beta_{m+1}T^{\frac{m+1}{M}},

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.11
.
Finally,

\displaystyle\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}=\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\leq\frac{400}{3}C^{4}\log{T}\cdot\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)=\frac{2}{5}T\leq T.

Combining all above we know Algorithm
2
is feasible, i.e.,
1<\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}<...<\beta_{M-1}T^{\frac{M-1}{M}}<T
.

Next, due to Lemma
10.27
, conditional on
\mathcal{E}
that happens with probability at least
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\mathcal{E})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}\leq 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}.

(72)

On the other hand, on the low probability event
\overline{\mathcal{E}}
that happens with probability at most
\frac{4}{T^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{V(T(1),T(0)|\overline{\mathcal{E}})}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}=

\displaystyle\ \frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(1)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}+\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}(0)}{(\sigma(1)+\sigma(0))^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \max\left\{\frac{T}{T-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}},\frac{T}{\frac{1}{2}\beta_{1}T^{\frac{1}{M}}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 2\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{1-\frac{1}{M}},

(73)

where the inequality is due to Lemma
9.1
.

So overall we have

\displaystyle\ \sup_{\sigma(1),\sigma(0)}\ \frac{\mathrm{E}[V(T(1),T(0))]}{V(T^{*}(1),T^{*}(0))}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \left(1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}\right)\left(1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}\right)+\frac{4}{T^{2}}\cdot 2\beta_{1}^{-1}T^{1-\frac{1}{M}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+96\cdot\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}+\frac{3}{50}C^{-4}(\log{T})^{-1}\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}T^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ 1+\left(96+\frac{3}{50}\frac{1}{(C^{4}\log{T})^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\ 1+97\left(\frac{1000}{3}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}C^{\frac{4(M-1)}{M}}T^{-\frac{M-1}{M}}(\log{T})^{\frac{M-1}{M}},

where the first inequality is using the total law of probability, and upper bounding the two parts using (
72
) and (
73
);
the second probability is upper bounding
1-\frac{4}{T^{2}}
by 1;
the third inequality is because
\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{M}}\leq\left(\frac{T}{\frac{1000}{3}C^{4}\log{T}}\right)^{\frac{1}{M}}
;
the last inequality is because
C^{4}\log{T}\geq 1
.

\halmos
